    (`exec … = some s'`) and `compile p` is installed at `m.pc` in agreement with the
    IL state, then the trusted machine runs to the fall-through PC in a state that
    still has the code installed and agrees with `s'`.

    PROOF STRATEGY (deferred): structural induction on `p`.
      • ops: one `Rv64i.step`; the register/memory update matches `exec`'s.
      • `seq a b`: compose the sub-simulations (run `a`'s block, then `b`'s).
      • `ife`/`while`: the *only* flat-PC reasoning — discharge the fixed branch/`jal`
        offset arithmetic of the lowering once; `while` adds a measure induction on
        the back-edge, its body handled by the structural IH.
    This is the per-program cost `Hex0/Refine.lean` pays at the flat altitude — here
    amortised into the compiler, then reused by every program (e.g. T2 below). -/
theorem compile_sim
    {p : Stmt} {s s' : St} {m : Rv64i.State} {fuel : Nat} {L : Layout}
    (hcode : L.code = compile p)
    (hpc   : m.pc = L.codeBase)
    (hinst : Installed L m)
    (hag   : Agree L s m)
    (hsep  : NoSelfModify L p s)
    (hexec : exec fuel p s = some s') :
    ∃ k, (Rv64i.runFuel L.endPc k m).pc = L.endPc
       ∧ Installed L (Rv64i.runFuel L.endPc k m)
       ∧ Agree L s' (Rv64i.runFuel L.endPc k m) := by

380	    the footprint side condition forbids IL stores here. -/
381	def MachPriv (L : Layout) (holes : List Hole) (a : Word) : Prop :=
382	  memRange a L.codeBase L.blobLen ∨ ∃ h ∈ holes, memRange a h.1 h.2
383	def machPrivB (L : Layout) (holes : List Hole) (a : Word) : Bool :=
384	  memRangeB a L.codeBase L.blobLen || holes.any (fun h => memRangeB a h.1 h.2)
385	
386	/-- The whole shared stack region `[stackLo, sp0)` — used by `prog_sim`'s
387	    coarse top-level memory agreement (the harness's output buffers live OUTSIDE
388	    it, so "agree off blob and off stack" is the composable observable; the
389	    per-hole `MachPriv` is the finer invariant `StInv` carries mid-run). -/
390	def MachStack (stackLo sp0 : Word) (a : Word) : Prop :=
391	  memRange a stackLo (sp0.toNat - stackLo.toNat)
392	
393	/-- **C2 — `OffPriv L holes sp a`**: `a` is neither machine-private (blob + live
394	    holes) NOR in the free stack `[stackLo, sp)` below the current frame. This is
395	    the domain on which IL and machine memory agree (`StInv`), and the region an
396	    IL memory op must stay inside (`MemAccOff`). The free-stack carve-out is what
397	    makes the caller's invariant RESTORABLE after a call returns: the machine
398	    dirties the callee's `[ra][slots]` there, and the IL never wrote it — but it
399	    is below `sp`, so it is outside `OffPriv` and agreement is not demanded. -/
400	def OffPriv (L : Layout) (holes : List Hole) (sp a : Word) : Prop :=
401	  ¬ MachPriv L holes a ∧ ¬ memRange a L.stackLo (sp.toNat - L.stackLo.toNat)
402	
403	/-- **`StInv L fd holes s m`** — the per-statement simulation invariant
404	    (RESUME-PROGSIM §3.1). At every IL statement boundary during `fd`'s body:
405	    `sp ≡ x2`; each live IL register sits in its 8-byte machine slot at
406	    `sp + slotOff r`; the code+data is installed; IL and machine memory agree
407	    off the machine-private bytes; and the current activation's hole is
408	    `[sp, sp + userOff fd)` with `sp` 8-aligned. -/
409	def StInv (L : Layout) (fd : FunDef) (holes : List Hole) (s : St) (m : State) : Prop :=

300	
301	/-- Compile a whole program. The stream is `jal ra, entry` (the 4-byte stub at
302	    offset 0), a `jal x0, 0` self-loop landing pad at offset 4 — the HALT
303	    address, occupied by a real instruction so no function entry can collide
304	    with it — then every function in env order, then (in `progBytes`) the
305	    data segment. Run the result with `runFuel (codeBase+4)`. `none` =
306	    ill-formed program, per-function limits exceeded, missing entry, data too
307	    large, or a branch/jump/cref out of range. The `T` variant also returns
308	    the function and data byte-offset tables. -/
309	def compileProgT (P : Program) (entry : Name) :
310	    Option (List Instr × List (Name × Nat) × List (Name × Nat)) :=
311	  if !(wfProgram P && P.env.all (fun nf => fnOk nf.2)
312	       && (List.lookup entry P.env).isSome
313	       && P.data.all (fun d => d.2.length < 2 ^ 22))    -- cref/clen synth range
314	       -- NOTE: bound tightened 2^23 → 2^22 (2026-07-05). `clen` synthesizes the
315	       -- data length via `synthConst` (no range check), whose hi immediate is
316	       -- `synthHi len = ⌊(len+2048)/4096⌋`; that hits 2048 for `len ∈ [2^23−2048,
317	       -- 2^23)`, overflowing `BitVec.ofInt 12` (wraps to −2048 ⇒ wrong length).
318	       -- 2^22 sits well inside the synthesizable band; discharges ProgSim `hdat`.
319	  then none
320	  else
321	    let segs : List (Name × List SymInstr) :=
322	      (P.env.mapM (fun nf => do pure (nf.1, ← compileFun P.data nf.2)) : M _).run' 0
323	    let (flat, lbls, fns, codeEnd) :=
324	      layout (("", [.callf entry, .ins (jal0 0)]) :: segs) 0
325	    -- SAME layout function as the IL harness (`Prog.dbaseOf`/`installData`
326	    -- use `dataOffsetsFrom 0`); correspondence proved in `Prog.dataSegment_at`.
327	    let dats := dataOffsetsFrom (pad8 codeEnd) P.data
328	    ((flat.mapM (resolveOne lbls fns dats)).map List.flatten).map
329	      (fun is => (is, fns.filter (fun f => f.1 != ""), dats))
330	
331	def compileProg (P : Program) (entry : Name) : Option (List Instr) :=
332	  (compileProgT P entry).map (·.1)
333	
334	/-- The full loadable blob: code, zero pad to 8, then the data segment
335	    (offsets per `dataOffsets`). This — not `compileProg`'s instruction list
336	    alone — is what runs when the program has data. -/
337	def progBytes (P : Program) (entry : Name) : Option (List Byte) :=
338	  (compileProg P entry).map fun is =>
339	    let code := LowIR.asmBytes is
340	    code ++ List.replicate (pad8 code.length - code.length) 0 ++ dataSegment P.data
341	
342	end LowIR.Compile
343	
```

301	/-- Every label position fits the 21-bit `jal`/branch offset window (from byte 0,
302	    so target − here ∈ (−2²⁰, 2²⁰) whenever both ends are `< 2²⁰`). -/
303	def LabelsOk (brkPos contPos : List Nat) (epiPos : Nat) : Prop :=
304	  (∀ p ∈ brkPos, p < 2 ^ 20) ∧ (∀ p ∈ contPos, p < 2 ^ 20) ∧ epiPos < 2 ^ 20
305	
306	/-- Every `ife`'s else-skip branch fits the 13-bit branch window (`8 + 4·csize e`
307	    = the compiler's `resolveOne` guard `δ ≤ 4094`). `while`'s branch is a fixed
308	    `+8`, always fine. A structural side condition, discharged per-program by the
309	    real layout (branches that don't fit make `Compile` return `none`). -/
310	def BranchOk : PStmt → Prop
311	  | .seq a b          => BranchOk a ∧ BranchOk b
312	  | .block body       => BranchOk body
313	  | .ife _ _ _ t e    => 8 + 4 * csize e < 2 ^ 12 ∧ BranchOk t ∧ BranchOk e
314	  | .while _ _ _ body => BranchOk body
315	  | _                 => True
316	
317	/-- A conditional branch `condInstr c T0 T1 δ` whose registers hold `x`, `y`: when
318	    `evalCond c x y` holds the branch is taken (`pc += δ`), matching the compiler's
319	    positive-form encoding (`.ge`/`.geu` take the *else* arm of `bge`/`bgeu`). -/
320	theorem cond_taken (m : State) (c : Cond) (δ : Int) (x y : Word)
321	    (hd : decode (fetch32 m) = condInstr c T0 T1 δ)
322	    (hx : m.rget T0 = x) (hy : m.rget T1 = y) (hev : evalCond c x y = true) :
323	    step m = m.setPc (m.pc + (BitVec.ofInt 13 δ).signExtend 64) := by
324	  cases c <;> simp only [condInstr] at hd
325	  · have h : x = y := by simpa [evalCond] using hev
326	    rw [step_beq m T0 T1 _ hd, hx, hy, if_pos h]
327	  · have h : x.slt y = true := by simpa [evalCond] using hev
328	    rw [step_blt m T0 T1 _ hd, hx, hy, if_pos h]
329	  · have h : x.slt y = false := by simpa [evalCond] using hev
330	    rw [step_bge m T0 T1 _ hd, hx, hy, if_neg (by simp [h])]
331	  · have h : x.ult y = false := by simpa [evalCond] using hev
332	    rw [step_bgeu m T0 T1 _ hd, hx, hy, if_neg (by simp [h])]
333	
334	/-- …and when `evalCond c x y` fails the branch falls through (`pc += 4`). -/
335	theorem cond_not_taken (m : State) (c : Cond) (δ : Int) (x y : Word)
336	    (hd : decode (fetch32 m) = condInstr c T0 T1 δ)
337	    (hx : m.rget T0 = x) (hy : m.rget T1 = y) (hev : evalCond c x y = false) :
338	    step m = m.setPc (m.pc + 4) := by
339	  cases c <;> simp only [condInstr] at hd
340	  · have h : ¬ x = y := by simpa [evalCond] using hev
341	    rw [step_beq m T0 T1 _ hd, hx, hy, if_neg h]
342	  · have h : x.slt y = false := by simpa [evalCond] using hev
343	    rw [step_blt m T0 T1 _ hd, hx, hy, if_neg (by simp [h])]
344	  · have h : x.slt y = true := by simpa [evalCond] using hev
345	    rw [step_bge m T0 T1 _ hd, hx, hy, if_pos h]
346	  · have h : x.ult y = true := by simpa [evalCond] using hev
347	    rw [step_bgeu m T0 T1 _ hd, hx, hy, if_pos h]
348

352	    l.getD k 0 < b := by
353	  induction l generalizing k with
354	  | nil => simpa using hb
355	  | cons x t ih =>
356	    cases k with
357	    | zero => simpa using h x (by simp)
358	    | succ k => simpa using ih k (fun p hp => h p (by simp [hp]))

183	def fnOk (fd : FunDef) : Bool :=

185	
186	/-- Prologue: drop sp, save ra, park params from a0.., `sd x0` every other
187	    slot (matches IL register-file zeroing EXACTLY), materialize the user-frame
188	    base into frameReg's slot, then zero the user frame `[sp+userOff, sp+totalFrame)`
189	    word-by-word (matches IL `frameEnter`'s `zeroRange` — the machine half of the
190	    zero-init decision; a callee reads 0, not a returned sibling's stale slots). -/
191	def prologue (fd : FunDef) : List SymInstr :=
192	  let tf := totalFrame fd
193	  let params := fd.params.toList
194	  [.ins (.addi SP SP (BitVec.ofInt 12 (-(tf : Int)))),
195	   .ins (.sd SP RA 0)]
196	  ++ params.zipIdx.flatMap (fun pi => storeSlot pi.1 (A pi.2))
197	  ++ ((List.range (maxRegF fd + 1)).filter
198	        (fun r => r != 0 && !params.contains r && r != fd.frameReg)).map
199	       (fun r => .ins (.sd SP 0 (BitVec.ofNat 12 (slotOff r))))
200	  ++ (if fd.frameReg = 0 then [] else
201	        [.ins (.addi T0 SP (BitVec.ofNat 12 (userOff fd)))] ++ storeSlot fd.frameReg T0)
202	  ++ (List.range (fd.frameSize / 8)).map
203	       (fun i => .ins (.sd SP 0 (BitVec.ofNat 12 (userOff fd + 8 * i))))
204	
205	/-- Epilogue (jumped to by `ret`, fallen into on `normal`): rets → a0..,
206	    restore ra + sp, return. -/
207	def epilogue (fd : FunDef) : List SymInstr :=
208	  fd.rets.toList.zipIdx.flatMap (fun ri => loadSlot ri.1 (A ri.2))
209	  ++ [.ins (.ld RA SP 0),
210	      .ins (.addi SP SP (BitVec.ofNat 12 (totalFrame fd))),
211	      .ins (.jalr 0 RA 0)]
212	
213	/-- Compile one function to a symbolic stream. -/
214	def compileFun (dat : Data) (fd : FunDef) : M (List SymInstr) := do
215	  let epi ← fresh
216	  let body ← lower dat [] [] epi fd.body
217	  pure <| prologue fd ++ body ++ [.label epi] ++ epilogue fd
218	
219	/-! ### Layout & resolution -/
220	
221	/-- Emitted size of a symbolic instruction (fixed per constructor — layout
222	    must be value-independent). `cref` = jal-pc-read + 3-instr delta synth +
223	    add (see `resolveOne`). -/
224	def symSize : SymInstr → Nat
225	  | .label _ => 0
226	  | .cref _  => 20
227	  | _        => 4
228	
229	/-- Pass A over one stream from byte position `pos`: positioned items,
230	    label→addr entries, next position. -/
231	def layoutItems : List SymInstr → Nat → List (Nat × SymInstr) × List (Nat × Nat) × Nat
232	  | [], pos => ([], [], pos)
233	  | .label l :: rest, pos =>
234	      let (flat, lbls, pos') := layoutItems rest pos
235	      ((pos, .label l) :: flat, (l, pos) :: lbls, pos')
236	  | si :: rest, pos =>
237	      let (flat, lbls, pos') := layoutItems rest (pos + symSize si)
238	      ((pos, si) :: flat, lbls, pos')

theorem compileProgT_dataBound {P : Program} {entry : Name} {t}
    (h : compileProgT P entry = some t) : P.data.all (fun d => d.2.length < 2 ^ 22) := by
  unfold compileProgT at h
  cases hg : (LowIR.Prog.wfProgram P && P.env.all (fun nf => LowIR.Compile.fnOk nf.2)
       && (List.lookup entry P.env).isSome
       && P.data.all (fun d => d.2.length < 2 ^ 22)) with
  | false => rw [hg] at h; simp at h
  | true => simp only [Bool.and_eq_true] at hg; exact hg.2

33	theorem resolveOne_length (lbls fns dats : List _) (pos : Nat) (si : SymInstr) (v : List Instr)
34	    (h : resolveOne lbls fns dats (pos, si) = some v) : 4 * v.length = symSize si := by

37	  | ins i => rw [← Option.some.inj h]; rfl
38	  | br c a b l =>
39	      cases hlk : List.lookup l lbls with

252	def wf (P : Program) : Nat → Nat → Stmt → Bool
253	  | bD, lD, .seq a b          => wf P bD lD a && wf P bD lD b
254	  | bD, lD, .ife _ _ _ t e    => wf P bD lD t && wf P bD lD e
255	  | bD, lD, .while _ _ _ body => wf P bD (lD+1) body
256	  | bD, lD, .block body       => wf P (bD+1) lD body
257	  | bD, _,  .brkB k           => k < bD
258	  | _,  lD, .contL k          => k < lD
259	  | _,  _,  .slli _ _ sh      => sh < 64
260	  | _,  _,  .srli _ _ sh      => sh < 64
261	  | _,  _,  .call argc rvc f _ _ =>
262	      match List.lookup f P.env with
263	      | some fd => fd.argc == argc && fd.rvc == rvc
264	      | none    => false
265	  | _,  _,  .cref _ d         => (List.lookup d P.data).isSome
266	  | _,  _,  .clen _ d         => (List.lookup d P.data).isSome
267	  | _,  _,  _                 => true
268	
269	/-- Program well-formedness: every body is `wf` with NO enclosing block/loop
270	    (an escaping brk/cont at function level is ill-formed), no function binds
271	    its `frameReg` as a parameter (frameReg would shadow the param binding),
272	    and data object names are unique. -/
273	def wfProgram (P : Program) : Bool :=
274	  P.env.all (fun nf =>
275	    wf P 0 0 nf.2.body && !(nf.2.params.toList.contains nf.2.frameReg))
276	  && (P.data.map Prod.fst).eraseDups.length == P.data.length
277	
278	/-- Data-free convenience (pre-data name, used by the existing tests). -/
279	def wfEnv (env : Env) : Bool := wfProgram env

218	/-- The pre-frame part of the resolved prologue (a direct transcription of
219	    `Compile.prologue` with `.ins` unwrapped and `storeSlot` → `storeSlotI`,
220	    minus the zero-frame segment): drop sp, save ra, park params, zero slots,
221	    materialize frameReg. -/
222	def prologuePreI (fd : FunDef) : List Instr :=
223	  let params := fd.params.toList
224	  [Instr.addi SP SP (BitVec.ofInt 12 (-(totalFrame fd : Int))), Instr.sd SP RA 0]
225	  ++ params.zipIdx.flatMap (fun pi => storeSlotI pi.1 (A pi.2))
226	  ++ ((List.range (maxRegF fd + 1)).filter
227	        (fun r => r != 0 && !params.contains r && r != fd.frameReg)).map
228	       (fun r => Instr.sd SP 0 (BitVec.ofNat 12 (slotOff r)))
229	  ++ (if fd.frameReg = 0 then [] else
230	        [Instr.addi T0 SP (BitVec.ofNat 12 (userOff fd))] ++ storeSlotI fd.frameReg T0)
231	
232	/-- The zero-frame segment (`sd SP 0` at each user-frame word offset) — the
233	    machine half of the zero-init frame agreement (RESUME-CALL ★). -/
234	def frameZeroI (fd : FunDef) : List Instr :=
235	  (List.range (fd.frameSize / 8)).map
236	    (fun i => Instr.sd SP 0 (BitVec.ofNat 12 (userOff fd + 8 * i)))
237	
238	def prologueI (fd : FunDef) : List Instr := prologuePreI fd ++ frameZeroI fd
239	
240	/-- The RESOLVED epilogue: rets → `a0..`, restore ra + sp, `jalr x0 ra 0`. -/
241	def epilogueI (fd : FunDef) : List Instr :=
242	  fd.rets.toList.zipIdx.flatMap (fun ri => loadSlotI ri.1 (A ri.2))
243	  ++ [Instr.ld RA SP 0, Instr.addi SP SP (BitVec.ofNat 12 (totalFrame fd)), Instr.jalr 0 RA 0]
244	
245	def prologueSize (fd : FunDef) : Nat := (prologueI fd).length
246	def epilogueSize (fd : FunDef) : Nat := fd.rvc + 3

=== loadSlotI def ===
def loadSlotI (r t : Nat) : List Instr :=
  if r = 0 then [.addi t 0 0] else [.ld t SP (BitVec.ofNat 12 (slotOff r))]

def storeSlotI (r t : Nat) : List Instr :=
  if r = 0 then [] else [.sd SP t (BitVec.ofNat 12 (slotOff r))]
```

=== resolveOne ===
def resolveOne (lbls : List (Nat × Nat)) (fns : List (Name × Nat))
    (dats : List (Name × Nat)) :
    Nat × SymInstr → Option (List Instr)
  | (_,   .label _)      => some []
  | (_,   .ins i)        => some [i]
  | (pos, .cref d)       => do
      let off ← List.lookup d dats
      let δ : Int := (off : Int) - ((pos : Int) + 4)
      let lo : Int := ((δ + 2048) % 4096) - 2048
      let hi : Int := (δ - lo) / 4096
      if -2048 ≤ hi && hi ≤ 2047 then
        some [.jal T0 (BitVec.ofInt 21 4),
              .addi T1 0 (BitVec.ofInt 12 hi), .slli T1 T1 12,
              .addi T1 T1 (BitVec.ofInt 12 lo),
              .add T0 T0 T1]
      else none
  | (pos, .br c a b l)   => do
      let tgt ← List.lookup l lbls
      let δ : Int := (tgt : Int) - (pos : Int)
      if -4096 ≤ δ && δ ≤ 4094 then some [condInstr c a b δ] else none
  | (pos, .jmp l)        => do
      let tgt ← List.lookup l lbls
      let δ : Int := (tgt : Int) - (pos : Int)
      if -(2^20 : Int) ≤ δ && δ ≤ (2^20 : Int) - 2 then some [jal0 δ] else none
  | (pos, .callf f)      => do
      let tgt ← List.lookup f fns
      let δ : Int := (tgt : Int) - (pos : Int)
      if -(2^20 : Int) ≤ δ && δ ≤ (2^20 : Int) - 2
      then some [.jal RA (BitVec.ofInt 21 δ)] else none

def pad8 (n : Nat) : Nat := (n + 7) / 8 * 8

def layout : List (Name × List SymInstr) →
    (start : Nat) →
    List (Nat × SymInstr) × List (Nat × Nat) × List (Name × Nat) × Nat
  | [], pos => ([], [], [], pos)
  | (n, items) :: rest, pos =>
      let (flat1, lbls1, pos') := layoutItems items pos
      let (flat2, lbls2, fns, endP) := layout rest pos'
      (flat1 ++ flat2, lbls1 ++ lbls2, (n, pos) :: fns, endP)

/-- Resolve one positioned symbolic instruction (range-checked). `cref d`:
    `jal T0, +4` reads the pc (T0 := address of the next instruction — pc-read
    with NO auipc, staying inside the 16-encoding surface and keeping the blob
    position-independent), then a fixed 3-instr synth of the delta to the data
    object into T1, then `add T0, T0, T1`. -/
def resolveOne (lbls : List (Nat × Nat)) (fns : List (Name × Nat))
=== lookup_filter_ne ===
/-- Looking up a non-`""` key is unaffected by dropping the `""`-keyed entries
    (the `L.fnTab` filter): the first match is preserved. -/
theorem lookup_filter_ne {β} (f : Name) (hf : f ≠ "") :
    ∀ (l : List (Name × β)), List.lookup f (l.filter (fun x => x.1 != "")) = List.lookup f l := by
  intro l
  induction l with
  | nil => rfl
  | cons x rest ih =>
    obtain ⟨k, v⟩ := x
    by_cases hk : k = ""
    · subst hk
      rw [List.filter_cons_of_neg (by simp)]
      simp only [List.lookup_cons, (show (f == "") = false by simpa using hf)]
      exact ih
    · rw [List.filter_cons_of_pos (by simpa using hk)]
      simp only [List.lookup_cons]
      cases hfk : f == k with
      | true => rfl
      | false => exact ih

/-- No `""`-keyed entry survives the `L.fnTab` filter, so `""` looks up to `none`. -/
theorem lookup_filter_empty {β} :
    ∀ (l : List (Name × β)), List.lookup "" (l.filter (fun x => x.1 != "")) = none := by
  intro l
  induction l with
  | nil => rfl
  | cons x rest ih =>
    obtain ⟨k, v⟩ := x
    by_cases hk : k = ""
    · subst hk; rw [List.filter_cons_of_neg (by simp)]; exact ih
    · rw [List.filter_cons_of_pos (by simpa using hk)]
      have hbk : (("" : Name) == k) = false := by
        cases hb : (("" : Name) == k)
        · rfl
        · rw [beq_iff_eq] at hb; exact absurd hb.symm hk
      simp only [List.lookup_cons, hbk]
      exact ih

/-- **`TabOk`.** The compiler's function/data tables agree with `dposOf`/`fnPosOf`.

303	def dataSegment : Data → List Byte
304	  | [] => []
305	  | (_, bs) :: rest =>

def memRange (a base : Word) (len : Nat) : Prop :=
  base.toNat ≤ a.toNat ∧ a.toNat < base.toNat + len
def memRangeB (a base : Word) (len : Nat) : Bool :=

def MachStack (stackLo sp0 : Word) (a : Word) : Prop :=

=== run def ===
def run (P : Program) (stackLo : Word) (fuel : Nat) (f : Name) (argVals : List Word)
    (mem : Word → Byte) (sp0 : Word) (pad : Name → Nat := fun _ => 0)
    (dataBase : Word := 0x30000) : Option St :=
  match List.lookup f P.env with
  | none => none
  | some fd =>
    let dbase := dbaseOf dataBase P.data
    let mem'  := installData dataBase P.data mem
    match frameEnter stackLo fd (pad f) argVals mem' sp0 with
    | none => none
    | some st0 =>
      match exec P dbase pad stackLo fuel fd.body st0 with
      | some (s1, .normal) | some (s1, .ret) => some s1
      | _ => none

theorem exec_call_inv (fuel argc rvc : Nat) (f : Name) (args : Vector Reg argc)
    (rets : Vector Reg rvc) (s s' : St) (oc : Outcome)
    (h : exec P dbase pad stackLo (fuel+1) (.call argc rvc f args rets) s = some (s', oc)) :
    ∃ (fd : FunDef) (callee s1 : St) (ocb : Outcome),
      List.lookup f P.env = some fd
      ∧ (fd.argc == argc && fd.rvc == rvc) = true
      ∧ frameEnter stackLo fd (pad f) (args.toList.map s.rget) s.mem s.sp = some callee
      ∧ exec P dbase pad stackLo fuel fd.body callee = some (s1, ocb)
      ∧ (ocb = Outcome.normal ∨ ocb = Outcome.ret)
      ∧ s' = (rets.toList.zip (fd.rets.toList.map s1.rget)).foldl
                (fun st rv => st.rset rv.1 rv.2) { s with mem := s1.mem }
      ∧ oc = Outcome.normal := by
  simp only [exec] at h
  split at h
  · simp at h
  · next fd hlk =>
    split at h
    · next harity =>
      split at h
      · next hfe => simp at h
      · next callee hfe =>
=== frameEnter ===
def frameEnter (stackLo : Word) (fd : FunDef) (pad : Nat) (argVals : List Word)
    (mem : Word → Byte) (spCaller : Word) : Option St :=
  if spCaller.toNat < stackLo.toNat + fd.frameSize + pad then none
  else
    let frameBase := spCaller - BitVec.ofNat 64 fd.frameSize
    let newSp := frameBase - BitVec.ofNat 64 pad
    let withParams : Reg → Word :=
      (fd.params.toList.zip argVals).foldl
        (fun rf pv => fun r => if r = pv.1 then pv.2 else rf r) (fun _ => 0)
    some { regs := fun r => if r = fd.frameReg then frameBase else withParams r
           mem  := zeroRange mem frameBase fd.frameSize
           sp   := newSp }

/-- Clocked big-step semantics with outcomes. Ctrl's equations verbatim for the
    shared constructs; every recursive call is at `fuel` from `fuel+1`.

    `call`: lookup + arity check (`wf` guarantees them; dynamic `none` keeps the
    semantics total), enter the frame, run the body accepting `normal`/`ret`
    (an escaping brk/cont is `none` — `wf` bans it), then return to the CALLER's
    registers and `sp` (structural restore), with only `rets` copied back and
    the callee's memory kept. -/
def exec (P : Program) (dbase : Name → Option Word) (pad : Name → Nat)
```

32	/-! ## Call-marshalling emit helpers (arg loads / ret stores).
33	
34	    A `call` lowers to: one `loadSlotI arg[i] (A i)` per argument (always 1
35	    instruction each, incl. reg 0 → `addi (A i) x0 0`), then `jal RA` to the
36	    callee, then one `storeSlotI ret[i] (A i)` per RETURN (0 instructions for
37	    ret reg 0). These mirror the compiler's `lower` call arm exactly (`A i` =
38	    `x10+i`), validated by `matchesRealProg` below. -/
39	def marshalI (args : List Nat) : List Instr :=
40	  args.zipIdx.flatMap fun ri => loadSlotI ri.1 (A ri.2)
41	
42	def retStoresI (rets : List Nat) : List Instr :=
43	  rets.zipIdx.flatMap fun ri => storeSlotI ri.1 (A ri.2)

45	/-- Each `loadSlotI` is one instruction, so `marshalI`'s length is the argument
46	    count (independent of the `A`-register indices). -/

Nat induction, no global pc-invariant needed since `hne` is a hypothesis.

`entry_run_sim` is the machine-side summit in plain-iteration (`stepN`) form — the whole run from the initial state through to the halt loop. Here's the statement (Main.lean:62-82):

```lean
theorem entry_run_sim
    {P : Program} {entry : Name} {fd : FunDef} {args : List Word}
    {stackLo sp0 : Word} {fuel : Nat} {s' : St} {L : Layout} {m0 : State}
    (hlk    : List.lookup entry P.env = some fd)
    (hL     : layoutOf P entry L.codeBase L.stackLo = some L)
    (hpre   : SimPre L stackLo sp0)
    (hpc    : m0.pc = L.codeBase)
    (hsp    : m0.rget 2 = sp0)
    (hargs  : ∀ i, i < fd.argc → m0.rget (10 + i) = args.getD i 0)
    (hinst  : Installed L m0)
    (hmem   : ∀ a, ¬ MachPriv L [] a →
                installData (L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 L.segStart) P.data (fun _ => 0) a
                  = m0.mem a)
    (hrun   : LowIR.Prog.run P stackLo fuel entry args (fun _ => 0) sp0
                (userPad P.env) (L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 L.segStart) = some s') :
    ∃ K, (stepN K m0).pc = L.haltPc
       ∧ (∀ j, j < fd.rvc → (stepN K m0).rget (10 + j) = s'.rget (fd.rets.toList.getD j 0))
       ∧ (∀ a, ¬ memRange a L.codeBase L.blobLen → ¬ MachStack stackLo sp0 a →
            s'.mem a = (stepN K m0).mem a)
       ∧ (∀ j, j < K → (stepN j m0).pc ≠ L.haltPc)
```

## Reading it

**Hypotheses** — identical to `prog_sim`'s. The machine starts at `m0` with:
- `hpc`/`hsp` — pc = `codeBase`, sp = `sp0`;
- `hargs` — the `argc` arguments already in `a0..a7` (`x10+i`);
- `hinst`/`hmem` — the code+data blob is installed and machine memory agrees with the IL's `installData` image off the private region;
- `hrun` — the IL side actually succeeds: `run … = some s'` (with the P1 padding `userPad`, data based at `codeBase + segStart`).

**Conclusion** — there's a step count `K` such that after `K` plain steps (`stepN K m0`):
1. `.pc = L.haltPc` (= `codeBase + 4`) — the machine has reached the halt loop;
2. `entry`'s `rvc` return values sit in `a0..` and equal `s'`'s values in the return registers;
3. machine and IL memory agree everywhere off the blob and off the stack;
4. **`∀ j < K, (stepN j m0).pc ≠ L.haltPc`** — the no-early-halt clause: the halt PC is reached *only* at step `K`, never before.

## Why the two differences from `prog_sim`

Those are exactly what make `entry_run_sim` the *provable-in-isolation* core, with `prog_sim` a thin corollary:

- **`stepN` instead of `runFuel`** — conclusion (1)–(3) are stated over plain iteration `stepN K m0`, not the halt-checking `runFuel L.haltPc k m0`. This is the RESUME §3.2 discipline: prove with `step^[k]` (composes cleanly), convert at the very top.
- **The extra `hne` conjunct (4)** — this is the exact hypothesis `runFuel_eq_stepN` needs to bridge `stepN K` to `runFuel halt K`. `prog_sim` obtains all four conjuncts, feeds (4) to the bridge to rewrite `stepN K m0 → runFuel L.haltPc K m0` in (1)–(3), and it's done.

So `entry_run_sim` carries the entire machine narrative (stub `jal` → prologue → body → epilogue → halt) *plus* the monotonicity fact that pc stays above `codeBase+4` until the final `jalr` returns — and that's the one piece still to prove.

## The two real uncertainties

1. **Memory-agreement threading** — establishing `prologue_sim`'s `hmemF`/`hcmemZ` (frame agreement at entry, from `hmem`'s `installData` image) and carrying `StInv`'s off-`OffPriv` agreement out through the epilogue. 2. **The `hne` no-early-halt clause** — this is the genuinely novel part, and potentially the largest single chunk if done directly (a per-step "pc stays in `[codeBase+8, codeBase+blobLen)` until the final `jalr`" invariant, which the sim lemmas don't currently expose).
